   136→
   137→// --- request/response conversion ---
   138→
   139→func (m *openaiModel) doRequest(ctx context.Context, req *model.LLMRequest) (*model.LLMResponse, error) {
   140→	msgs := convertContents(req)
   141→	tools := convertTools(req)
   142→
   143→	chatReq := chatRequest{
   144→		Model:    m.name,
   145→		Messages: msgs,
   146→		Tools:    tools,
   147→		Stream:   false,
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	if req.Config != nil {
   151→		chatReq.Temperature = req.Config.Temperature
   152→		chatReq.TopP = req.Config.TopP
   153→		if req.Config.MaxOutputTokens > 0 {
   154→			chatReq.MaxTokens = req.Config.MaxOutputTokens
   155→		}
   156→		if len(req.Config.StopSequences) > 0 {
   157→			chatReq.Stop = req.Config.StopSequences
   158→		}
   159→	}
   160→
   161→	if hasWebSearch(req) {
   162→		chatReq.WebSearchOptions = &webSearchOptions{}
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	body, err := json.Marshal(chatReq)
   166→	if err != nil {
   167→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: marshal request: %w", err)
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	httpReq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, m.baseURL+"/chat/completions", bytes.NewReader(body))
   171→	if err != nil {
   172→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: create http request: %w", err)
   173→	}
   174→	httpReq.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   175→	httpReq.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+m.apiKey)
   176→
   177→	httpResp, err := m.client.Do(httpReq)
   178→	if err != nil {
   179→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: http request: %w", err)
   180→	}
   181→	defer httpResp.Body.Close()
   182→
   183→	respBody, err := io.ReadAll(httpResp.Body)
   184→	if err != nil {
   185→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: read response: %w", err)
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	if httpResp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
   189→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: API returned status %d: %s", httpResp.StatusCode, string(respBody))
   190→	}
   191→
   192→	var chatResp chatResponse
   193→	if err := json.Unmarshal(respBody, &chatResp); err != nil {
   194→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: unmarshal response: %w", err)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	if chatResp.Error != nil {
   198→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: API error: %s (type=%s, code=%s)",
   199→			chatResp.Error.Message, chatResp.Error.Type, chatResp.Error.Code)
   200→	}
   201→
   202→	return convertResponse(&chatResp), nil
   203→}
   204→
   205→// convertContents transforms ADK genai.Content messages into OpenAI chat messages.
   206→func convertContents(req *model.LLMRequest) []chatMessage {
   207→	var msgs []chatMessage
   208→
   209→	// System instruction.
   210→	if req.Config != nil && req.Config.SystemInstruction != nil {
   211→		var text string
   212→		for _, p := range req.Config.SystemInstruction.Parts {
   213→			if p.Text != "" {
   214→				text += p.Text
   215→			}
   216→		}
   217→		if text != "" {
   218→			msgs = append(msgs, chatMessage{Role: "system", Content: text})
   219→		}
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	// Conversation contents.
   223→	for _, c := range req.Contents {
   224→		role := mapRole(c.Role)
   225→
   226→		// Check each part: could be text, function_call, or function_response.
   227→		for _, p := range c.Parts {
   228→			switch {
   229→			case p.FunctionCall != nil:
   230→				argsJSON, _ := json.Marshal(p.FunctionCall.Args)
   231→				msgs = append(msgs, chatMessage{
   232→					Role: "assistant",
   233→					ToolCalls: []toolCall{{
   234→						ID:   p.FunctionCall.ID,
   235→						Type: "function",
   236→						Function: functionCall{
   237→							Name:      p.FunctionCall.Name,
   238→							Arguments: string(argsJSON),
   239→						},
   240→					}},
   241→				})
   242→			case p.FunctionResponse != nil:
   243→				respJSON, _ := json.Marshal(p.FunctionResponse.Response)
   244→				msgs = append(msgs, chatMessage{
   245→					Role:       "tool",
   246→					Content:    string(respJSON),
   247→					ToolCallID: p.FunctionResponse.ID,
   248→				})
   249→			case p.Text != "":
   250→				msgs = append(msgs, chatMessage{Role: role, Content: p.Text})
   251→			}
   252→		}
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	// Merge consecutive assistant messages with tool_calls.
   256→	msgs = mergeAssistantToolCalls(msgs)
   257→
   258→	return msgs
   259→}
   260→
   261→// mergeAssistantToolCalls combines adjacent assistant messages that each have
   262→// tool_calls into a single message, as OpenAI expects multiple tool_calls in
   263→// one message rather than separate messages.
   264→func mergeAssistantToolCalls(msgs []chatMessage) []chatMessage {
   265→	if len(msgs) == 0 {
   266→		return msgs
   267→	}
   268→	var merged []chatMessage
   269→	for _, msg := range msgs {
   270→		if msg.Role == "assistant" && len(msg.ToolCalls) > 0 && len(merged) > 0 {
   271→			prev := &merged[len(merged)-1]
   272→			if prev.Role == "assistant" && len(prev.ToolCalls) > 0 {
   273→				prev.ToolCalls = append(prev.ToolCalls, msg.ToolCalls...)
   274→				continue
   275→			}
   276→		}
   277→		merged = append(merged, msg)
   278→	}
   279→	return merged
   280→}
   281→
   282→// hasWebSearch checks whether the request contains a GoogleSearch tool marker,
   283→// indicating the caller wants web search capability.
   284→func hasWebSearch(req *model.LLMRequest) bool {
   285→	if req.Config == nil {
   286→		return false
   287→	}
   288→	for _, t := range req.Config.Tools {
   289→		if t != nil && t.GoogleSearch != nil {
   290→			return true
   291→		}
   292→	}
   293→	return false
   294→}
   295→
   296→// convertTools transforms ADK genai.Tool definitions into OpenAI tool format.
   297→func convertTools(req *model.LLMRequest) []chatTool {
   298→	if req.Config == nil || len(req.Config.Tools) == 0 {
   299→		return nil
   300→	}
   301→
   302→	var tools []chatTool
   303→	for _, t := range req.Config.Tools {
   304→		if t.GoogleSearch != nil {
   305→			continue
   306→		}
   307→		for _, fd := range t.FunctionDeclarations {
   308→			ct := chatTool{
   309→				Type: "function",
   310→				Function: chatFunction{
   311→					Name:        fd.Name,
   312→					Description: fd.Description,
   313→				},
   314→			}
   315→			if fd.Parameters != nil {
   316→				ct.Function.Parameters = schemaToMap(fd.Parameters)
   317→			}
   318→			tools = append(tools, ct)
   319→		}
   320→	}
   321→	return tools
   322→}
   323→
   324→// schemaToMap converts a genai.Schema to a JSON-serialisable map that matches
   325→// the OpenAI function parameters format (JSON Schema).
   326→func schemaToMap(s *genai.Schema) map[string]any {
   327→	m := map[string]any{}
   328→	if s.Type != "" {
   329→		m["type"] = strings.ToLower(string(s.Type))
   330→	}
   331→	if s.Description != "" {
   332→		m["description"] = s.Description
   333→	}
   334→	if len(s.Enum) > 0 {
   335→		m["enum"] = s.Enum
   336→	}
   337→	if s.Items != nil {
   338→		m["items"] = schemaToMap(s.Items)
   339→	}
   340→	if len(s.Properties) > 0 {
   341→		props := map[string]any{}
   342→		for k, v := range s.Properties {
   343→			props[k] = schemaToMap(v)
   344→		}
   345→		m["properties"] = props
   346→	}
   347→	if len(s.Required) > 0 {
   348→		m["required"] = s.Required
   349→	}
   350→	return m
   351→}
   352→
   353→// convertResponse transforms an OpenAI chat completion response into an ADK LLMResponse.
   354→func convertResponse(resp *chatResponse) *model.LLMResponse {
   355→	llmResp := &model.LLMResponse{
   356→		TurnComplete: true,
   357→	}
   358→
   359→	if len(resp.Choices) == 0 {
   360→		llmResp.Content = genai.NewContentFromText("", "model")
   361→		return llmResp
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	choice := resp.Choices[0]
   365→	llmResp.FinishReason = mapFinishReason(choice.FinishReason)
   366→
   367→	content := &genai.Content{Role: "model"}
   368→
   369→	// Text content.
   370→	if choice.Message.Content != "" {
   371→		content.Parts = append(content.Parts, &genai.Part{Text: choice.Message.Content})
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	// Tool calls.
   375→	for _, tc := range choice.Message.ToolCalls {
   376→		var args map[string]any
   377→		if tc.Function.Arguments != "" {
   378→			_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(tc.Function.Arguments), &args)
   379→		}
   380→		content.Parts = append(content.Parts, &genai.Part{
   381→			FunctionCall: &genai.FunctionCall{
   382→				ID:   tc.ID,
   383→				Name: tc.Function.Name,
   384→				Args: args,
   385→			},
   386→		})
   387→	}
   388→
   389→	llmResp.Content = content
   390→
   391→	if resp.Usage != nil {
   392→		llmResp.UsageMetadata = &genai.GenerateContentResponseUsageMetadata{
   393→			PromptTokenCount:     resp.Usage.PromptTokens,
   394→			CandidatesTokenCount: resp.Usage.CompletionTokens,
   395→			TotalTokenCount:      resp.Usage.TotalTokens,
   396→		}
   397→	}
   398→
   399→	return llmResp
   400→}
   401→
   402→func mapRole(role string) string {
   403→	switch role {
   404→	case "model":
   405→		return "assistant"
   406→	default:
   407→		return role
   408→	}
   409→}
   410→
   411→func mapFinishReason(reason string) genai.FinishReason {
   412→	switch reason {
   413→	case "stop":
   414→		return genai.FinishReasonStop
   415→	case "length":
   416→		return genai.FinishReasonMaxTokens
   417→	case "tool_calls":
   418→		return genai.FinishReasonStop
   419→	case "content_filter":
   420→		return genai.FinishReasonSafety
   421→	default:
   422→		return genai.FinishReasonOther
   423→	}
   424→}
   425→

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"

    10→	"github.com/riverqueue/river"

    14→
    15→// AgentWorker is a River worker that processes AgentJobArgs by running the
    16→// LLM agent orchestrator. When a job is dequeued, it loads the corresponding
    17→// agent run from the store and delegates to Orchestrator.RunAgent.
    18→type AgentWorker struct {
    19→	river.WorkerDefaults[queue.AgentJobArgs]
    20→	orchestrator *Orchestrator
    21→	store        OrchestratorStore
    22→}
    23→
    24→// NewAgentWorker creates a new AgentWorker backed by the given orchestrator
    25→// and store.
    26→func NewAgentWorker(orchestrator *Orchestrator, store OrchestratorStore) *AgentWorker {
    27→	return &AgentWorker{
    28→		orchestrator: orchestrator,
    29→		store:        store,
    30→	}
    31→}
    32→
    33→// Work processes a single AgentJobArgs job. It loads the agent run from the
    34→// database and runs the LLM agent through the orchestrator. If the project
    35→// has WaitForAllSources enabled and not all sources have the target version,
    36→// the job is snoozed for 5 minutes via river.JobSnooze.
    37→func (w *AgentWorker) Work(ctx context.Context, job *river.Job[queue.AgentJobArgs]) error {
    38→	slog.Info("agent worker picked up job",
    39→		"job_id", job.ID,
    40→		"agent_run_id", job.Args.AgentRunID,
    41→		"attempt", job.Attempt,
    42→	)
    43→
    44→	run, err := w.store.GetAgentRun(ctx, job.Args.AgentRunID)
    45→	if err != nil {
    46→		slog.Error("agent worker failed to load agent run",
    47→			"job_id", job.ID,
    48→			"agent_run_id", job.Args.AgentRunID,
    49→			"err", err,
    50→		)
    51→		return fmt.Errorf("get agent run: %w", err)
    52→	}
    53→
    54→	// Check multi-source waiting: if the run has a version and the project
    55→	// has WaitForAllSources enabled, verify all sources have reported a
    56→	// release for the target version before running the agent.
    57→	if run.Version != "" {
    58→		project, err := w.store.GetProject(ctx, run.ProjectID)
    59→		if err != nil {
    60→			slog.Error("agent worker failed to load project",
    61→				"job_id", job.ID,
    62→				"project_id", run.ProjectID,
    63→				"err", err,
    64→			)
    65→			return fmt.Errorf("get project: %w", err)
    66→		}
    67→
    68→		var rules models.AgentRules
    69→		if len(project.AgentRules) > 0 {
    70→			if err := json.Unmarshal(project.AgentRules, &rules); err != nil {
    71→				slog.Warn("agent worker: failed to unmarshal agent rules, proceeding without wait",
    72→					"project_id", run.ProjectID, "err", err)
    73→			}
    74→		}
    75→
    76→		if rules.WaitForAllSources {
    77→			ready, err := w.orchestrator.checkAllSourcesReady(ctx, run.ProjectID, run.Version)
    78→			if err != nil {
    79→				slog.Error("agent worker: error checking source readiness",
    80→					"job_id", job.ID,
    81→					"project_id", run.ProjectID,
    82→					"version", run.Version,
    83→					"err", err,
    84→				)
    85→				return fmt.Errorf("check all sources ready: %w", err)
    86→			}
    87→			if !ready {
    88→				// After maxWaitAttempts (12 × 5min = ~1 hour), proceed
    89→				// with partial data rather than waiting forever.
    90→				const maxWaitAttempts = 12
    91→				if job.Attempt >= maxWaitAttempts {
    92→					slog.Warn("agent worker: max wait attempts reached, proceeding with partial data",
    93→						"job_id", job.ID,
    94→						"project_id", run.ProjectID,
    95→						"version", run.Version,
    96→						"attempts", job.Attempt,
    97→					)
    98→				} else {
    99→					slog.Info("agent worker: not all sources ready, snoozing",
   100→						"job_id", job.ID,
   101→						"project_id", run.ProjectID,
   102→						"version", run.Version,
   103→						"attempt", job.Attempt,
   104→					)
   105→					if err := w.store.UpdateAgentRunStatus(ctx, run.ID, "waiting"); err != nil {
   106→						slog.Error("failed to set waiting status", "err", err)
   107→					}
   108→					return river.JobSnooze(5 * time.Minute)
   109→				}
   110→			}
   111→		}
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	slog.Info("agent worker starting run",
   115→		"job_id", job.ID,
   116→		"agent_run_id", run.ID,
   117→		"project_id", run.ProjectID,
   118→		"trigger", run.Trigger,
   119→	)
   120→
   121→	if err := w.orchestrator.RunAgent(ctx, run); err != nil {
   122→		slog.Error("agent worker run failed",
   123→			"job_id", job.ID,
   124→			"agent_run_id", run.ID,
   125→			"project_id", run.ProjectID,
   126→			"err", err,
   127→		)
   128→		return err
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	slog.Info("agent worker run completed",
   132→		"job_id", job.ID,
   133→		"agent_run_id", run.ID,
   134→		"project_id", run.ProjectID,
   135→	)
   136→	return nil
   137→}
   138→

> AGENT

River's default job timeout is 60 seconds. The timeline in the logs confirms this: the first data_agent call started at `00:12:06` and the final error was at `00:12:49` — about 43 seconds. The root agent dispatched 4 parallel `data_agent` calls, each of which requires a full OpenAI round-trip internally (sub-agent → OpenAI → function calls → OpenAI → response). With 4 parallel calls that's a lot of concurrent OpenAI requests.

The problem is clear: **River's default 60-second job timeout is too short for the sub-agent architecture with OpenAI**. Each `data_agent` call needs the sub-agent to make its own LLM call, which can take 10-20+ seconds. With 4 parallel sub-agent invocations plus the root agent's own calls, 60 seconds isn't enough.

The fix is to override the `Timeout()` method on `AgentWorker` to give it a longer deadline. Let me also check if Gemini had similar issues with sub-agents.

No existing `Timeout()` overrides — both workers use River's default 60s. The fix is straightforward: add a `Timeout()` method to `AgentWorker` that returns a longer duration (e.g., 5 minutes) to accommodate the multi-step sub-agent LLM calls.

> AGENT

Fixed. The root cause was **River's default 60-second job timeout**. The unified sub-agent architecture requires multiple sequential LLM round-trips:

1. Root agent (`gpt-5.2`) dispatches 4 parallel `data_agent` calls
2. Each `data_agent` makes its own OpenAI call (sub-agent → OpenAI API → function tools → OpenAI API → response)
3. After data_agent responses, root agent may call `search_agent` (another OpenAI call)

With 4 parallel sub-agent invocations each needing 10-30 seconds, the 60-second job timeout was insufficient. The fix overrides `Timeout()` on `AgentWorker` to return 5 minutes, matching the scale of the multi-step agent workflow.
